SCENE TITLE: RAIN ON TIN, BREATH ON GLASS
Rain ticks a steady rhythm against the windowpane and the inn’s tin awning, a soft hiss folding into the room like a blanket. The
hearth down the hall is only a dull heat now—woodsmoke faded to a thin, warm ghost—while cold morning air sneaks in through old
frame seams and turns every breath into a faint, silver blur.
